# Part 6: tests/ that read the repository's own text, plus the helper modules

Scope: the test functions, and the module-level helpers they use, that read this repository's own documents, records, workflows, templates, config or code text and fail on what they find. The four helper modules `tests/conftest.py`, `tests/block_shapes.py`, `tests/commonmark_oracle.py` and `tests/gfm_table_oracle.py` are included whole. A test that only drives a script over `tmp_path` fixtures is out of scope. Those files are counted per file under *Size and growth*, and the skipped file names are listed at the end.

How the rows are grouped:
- A unit that parses something gets a row of its own: a corpus regex, an `ls-files` or glob walk, a table or section parse, an AST of code, or a comparison of two documents.
- Pure substring pins (`assert "<sentence>" in flat(read(doc))` / `not in`) are grouped one row per (file, target document, presence or absence). The Unit cell lists the test names without the `test_` prefix.

How the class is assigned:
- **guess**: a sentence a person wrote, read as a substring or by regex. Most rows in this part are this.
- **owned**: a marker the repository's own generator or validator writes, such as a `<!-- specs/<id> -->` line, the round-record field vocabulary, or a `seal/config.md` key.
- **observed**: an AST of Python source, `git ls-files`/`ls-tree`, `json.load`, an exit code, or a third-party renderer.

How the Unknown column is assigned:
- A presence pin is `refused`, because a rewording fails it.
- An absence pin is `passed`, because a reworded forbidden sentence slips through.

**Totals.**
- **Reader rows:** 785. This counts distinct reading units, after grouping pure substring pins one row per (file, document, presence or absence).
- **Classes:** guess 545, owned 145, observed 95.
- **Unknown input:** refused 502, mixed 178, passed 105.
- **Files with rows:** 131 test modules plus 3 helper modules. `tests/block_shapes.py` is data only and has no row.
- **Not counted:** test functions are counted as `def`s, so a parametrised test counts once.

**Skipped, with no in-scope reader** (fixture-only tests, 42 files):
- **Found by an AST scan plus a manual check:** test_a_commit_behind_a_wrapper_or_in_a_substitution_is_judged, test_a_creation_is_judged_before_git_runs, test_a_fix_of_a_fix_is_counted, test_a_path_the_command_wrote_out, test_a_signers_ci_prints_its_pact, test_a_workflow_is_read_the_one_way (it drives the conftest workflow helpers over fixtures), test_an_automation_run_meets_no_commit_prompt, test_chain_hooks, test_deferral_check, test_lease_liveness, test_lint_python, test_local_mode_resolves_under_the_git_dir, test_new_says_when_head_is_not_the_target, test_no_shape_the_base_stops_reads_silent, test_one_heredoc_shape_agrees_with_the_shell, test_one_heredoc_shape_is_data_to_the_commit_gate, test_review_skill_gate, test_session_cost, test_session_cost_post, test_the_commit_gate_decides_at_the_commit, test_the_fixes_close_the_record, test_the_hook_surface_git_offers, test_the_mode_question_is_asked_once, test_the_old_spellings_reach_the_hook, test_the_reader_agrees_with_bash, test_the_recorder_writes_what_its_process_ran, test_the_waiver_can_be_typed, test_version_check, test_what_the_reader_understands, test_worktree_guard, test_worktree_guard_signals.
- **Opened by a fork and found to hold no in-scope test:** test_a_script_copied_alone_exits_2, test_a_pact_review_takes_a_pact_change, test_local_mode_reaches_the_review_chain, test_two_branches_re_read_one_released_row, test_gates_do_not_fail_open, test_a_process_record_drop_passes_the_readers, test_dispatch, test_the_hooks_are_installed_where_git_runs_them, test_the_implementer_is_recorded, test_a_commit_behind_a_reserved_word_is_judged, test_console_is_not_utf8.

## Observations

- **Most of what these tests read is a person's prose.** Of 785 rows, 545 are `guess`, 145 `owned` and 95 `observed`. The largest cell is guess × refused (324): a sentence pinned verbatim, which fails on any rewording. Guess × passed (68) are absence pins. Each forbids one retired spelling, and a reworded copy of the forbidden claim goes through. The heaviest files are almost pure pin tables: `test_the_seal_is_taken_once_by_the_sealer.py` (37 tests), `test_first_setup_asks_once.py` (38, no fixture at all), `test_a_segment_feeds_the_flow_log.py` (35, about 50 sentence pins), `test_waiver_decided_at_start.py` (29), `test_the_rules_have_one_owner.py` (29) and `test_broad_gate_rule.py` (27).
- **Growth since v0.18.0 sits in the pin files, and their absence lists grow with every redesign.** `test_the_seal_is_taken_once_by_the_sealer.py` went from 5883 to 9934 lines (7 commits; #758 #787 #789 #807 #812–#818 #825 #832 #841 #846 #849 #851 #859). It holds about 20 retired words at `:3999-4020` and about 30 retired sentences at `:7660-7797`, and `unmeasured_word_says_so…` pins whole message sentences by equality. `test_a_released_row_is_read_again_in_a_fragment.py` went from 1743 to 4539 lines (5 commits). `test_the_stamp_reaches_the_person_it_is_drawn_for.py` went from 915 to 1160 lines (#832, #859). `test_one_word_one_meaning.py` went from 659 to 885 lines (5 commits).
- **The readers patched most often are exemption and classification tables.** Unrelated features edit them: `test_release_hygiene.py:130` `VERSIONS_OF_ANOTHER_PRODUCT` (8 commits; #764 added a git-build row, #825 dropped the pytest rows). It guesses this plugin's version from any dotted triple in prose. `test_one_word_one_meaning.py:725-765` `without_the_policy_span` rebuilds GFM paragraph termination for one exemption; #822 and #830/#831 each added a block kind. `test_every_reader_ends_a_line_where_gfm_does.py:606/:671` `OUT_OF_CLASS` (7 commits; about 60 `(path, unit): count` entries). `test_a_shrunken_corpus_declines_to_judge.py:154-235` `PATH_LIST_CALLS` (3 commits). Its AST machinery is copied at `test_a_corrected_sentence_survives_elsewhere.py:1129`.
- **Workflow YAML has one shared reader that most readers bypass.** `tests/conftest.py:51-160` (`code_line`, `workflow_steps`, `workflow_step`, `step_running`, #482) calls itself "the one reading". At least 14 test sites slice YAML their own way: by job keys, `"  pytest:"`…`"  ledger:"`: `test_the_windows_leg_runs_in_shards_that_make_the_whole.py:28`, `test_a_slow_case_names_itself.py`, `test_the_suite_has_a_command_that_is_cheap_twice.py:976/:1196`; by a six-space `- name:`: `test_the_release_check_watches_what_ships.py:79/:175`, `test_a_corrected_sentence_survives_elsewhere.py:2112`, `test_a_release_cannot_ship_an_untrue_milestone.py:428`, `test_a_declared_label_reaches_the_tracker.py:282`; by `rindex("- name:")`: `test_the_claude_md_block_has_one_source.py:403`; by a private job parser: `test_a_release_publishes_its_note.py:592`; by private comment strips: `test_a_merged_ticket_says_so_on_the_tracker.py:59`, `test_a_release_cannot_ship_an_untrue_milestone.py:554`; on raw text, where a commented-out step still counts: `test_release_hygiene.py:1932`, `test_a_body_naming_two_issues_claims_one.py`, `test_the_changelog_is_gathered_at_release.py`, `test_the_ledger_fragments_fold_at_release.py`.
- **One judgment is copied across several workflow tests.** The `^permissions:` scope regex appears three times: `test_a_merged_ticket_says_so_on_the_tracker.py:406`, `test_a_release_cannot_ship_an_untrue_milestone.py:556`, `test_release_hygiene.py:1435`. The `base_ref != main` guard is matched with at least five spellings: `test_the_release_check_watches_what_ships.py:259`, `test_the_gate_names_every_step_ci_runs.py:1092`, `test_the_ledger_fragments_fold_at_release.py:1156`, `test_a_release_cannot_ship_an_untrue_milestone.py`, `test_the_changelog_is_gathered_at_release.py`. The pip-install pins are read three times: `test_the_release_seal_is_drawn.py:819`, `test_the_suite_has_a_command_that_is_cheap_twice.py`, `test_arm_check.py`.
- **Copied locators behave differently from each other.** The `## §N` contract-section regex is defined separately in at least six files: `test_a_commit_names_its_repository.py:27` returns `(None,"")`, `test_the_seal_is_taken_once_by_the_sealer.py:9701` raises, and `test_a_moved_rule_leaves_its_definition.py:65`, `test_a_probe_that_commits_says_so.py:24`, `test_the_agent_contract_holds_the_universal_rules.py:24` and `test_broad_gate_rule.py` each have their own. 63 test modules define their own `read` and 42 their own `flat`. 18 define a `section`-style locator. The one-home pair already disagrees: `test_the_ledger_rules_have_one_home.py:104` reads `##` only, while `test_the_rules_claude_md_names_have_one_home.py:125` reads any level and stops at a sibling.
- **One rule, checked in several places.** The Python floor: `test_release_hygiene.py:1017`, `test_a_script_says_which_interpreter_it_needs.py:415`, `test_arm_check.py:200`, `test_the_suite_has_a_command_that_is_cheap_twice.py`. "Rounds are capped at **three**": `test_the_last_rounds_fixes_are_checked.py:1236`, `test_chain_hooks_hardening.py:1252`, `test_the_rules_have_one_owner.py:724`. `NOT for` in model-invoked skills: `test_chain_hooks_hardening.py:842` and `test_the_agent_contract_holds_the_universal_rules.py:123`. The `release/vX.Y.Z` token: `test_the_contributor_has_a_procedure.py:71,174,244` and `test_the_release_check_watches_what_ships.py:200`. The 15-word copy window: `test_the_seal_is_taken_once_by_the_sealer.py:9921` retypes `WINDOW = 15` (`:9685`) and checks a strict subset of `test_a_moved_rule_leaves_its_definition.py`. `imported_roots` is copied verbatim at `test_one_table_walker_reads_what_gfm_renders.py:342` and `test_the_hooks_hide_what_a_renderer_hides.py:39`, and its reach tuple has drifted (`.github/` vs `.github`).
- **Fence and heading detection are spelled several times.** Fence detection: `test_docs_line_wrap.py:84` (an lstrip-widened opener), `unverified_check.py`, `round_record.py#fenced_after`, `hooks/blocks.py#FENCE`, and the vendored pair in `evidence_check.py`. The `<!-- specs/… -->` marker regex is copied, not imported, at `test_docs_line_wrap.py:49` (from `unverified_check.py:117`). The "does this line start a block" heading regex appears in `round_record.py`, `issue_claims_check.py`, `survivor_check.py` and `conftest.py:gathered_entry`. Only two of them are held equal, by comparing their regex strings (`test_the_record_is_generated.py:539-561`). CommonMark ATX headings are re-derived by regex in `test_both_editions_carry_the_same_folds.py` (`HEADING`). Against all this, `tests/commonmark_oracle.py` and `tests/gfm_table_oracle.py` are the suite's only `observed` markdown readers, and they are held only to the hook readers, not to these test-side locators.
- **Readers that already derive the needle from what the repository generates.** These are owned or observed: module constants: `test_a_record_precedes_the_fixes_it_commissions.py:908/:1396` (`NOT_YET`, `WRITTEN_LATE`), `test_the_fixes_name_their_surface.py:714` (`call_sites` via AST), `test_the_pull_request_language_is_the_repositorys.py:1146` (checker literals); the real template run through the real parser: `test_waiver_decided_at_start.py` template_PARSES_*, `test_the_record_is_generated.py:2245`, `test_a_signer_records_a_pact_change.py:663`. Beside them sit hand-typed copies of the same kinds of value: `test_a_record_says_why_it_was_written_late.py:339` (`| Written late |`), `test_the_handoff_before_round_one.py:561-620` (a regex over the `SEGMENT_BARS` source formatting, though the module is importable), `test_the_fixes_name_their_surface.py:867` (the bare words `Contract changes`/`New units`, the failure that `test_a_record_says_what_ran_it.py:547` documents for `Ran by`), and the hand lists `ROUND_RECORD_FIELDS` (`test_the_pull_request_language_is_the_repositorys.py:1001`) and `COVERED` (`test_docs_line_wrap.py:117`).
- **Prose phrases and number words stand in for markers or counts.** `spawned for exactly that` decides ownership in `test_broad_gate_rule.py:only_one_definition_assigns_the_broad_gate` and `test_the_seal_is_taken_once_by_the_sealer.py:9897`. The four-clause `PINS` line every agent opens with (`test_every_agent_reads_the_contract.py:37`). "Eight of the eleven gates" in the READMEs (`test_the_stamp_reaches_the_person_it_is_drawn_for.py`). English number words for counts a glob already holds (`test_chain_hooks_hardening.py:318/:642`, `test_broad_gate_rule.py:sealer_counts_the_silent_definitions_from_the_glob`, "Five requirements" at `test_the_handoff_names_the_form_it_ran.py:68`). Numbers regexed back out of sentences that `seal/config.md` already holds (`test_a_document_has_room_for_the_next_fold.py:203-221`).
- **Some readers parse a tool's printed human output.** `test_the_ledger_cases_hold_under_a_latin_1_locale.py:72` reads pytest's `"N passed"` line, though broad_gate moved off printed pytest lines in #825. `test_the_reopening_is_one.py:566-590` reads chain_check's printed lines by substring, and skips unless `origin/release/v0.8.1` is fetched. `test_a_corrected_sentence_survives_elsewhere.py:paths_in` reads survivor_check's report lines.
- **Some anchors will break as the tree changes.** The survivor ranges pin exact coordinate sets from a prose-similarity score to bare SHAs (`test_a_corrected_sentence_survives_elsewhere.py:2613-2645`, `:4844`). Only the two `fixture/survivor-*` tags are guarded (`:119`), so a history rewrite turns the SHA cases into skips. `test_the_seal_is_taken_once_by_the_sealer.py:3892/:7843` read `seal/specs/<item>/changelog.md` directly, and both items are already gathered. `settle --retire` will raise FileNotFoundError there, which `test_the_changelog_is_gathered_at_release.py:558-574` avoids through `conftest.gathered_entry`. Fixed floors over corpora that a fold shrinks: `test_unverified_rows_close.py:1988` (≥90/≥80), `test_settle_reads_before_it_removes.py:1906` (>1000), `test_no_passage_is_pasted_into_a_second_file.py:304` (≥50), `test_a_pact_anchor_is_no_coordinate_of_the_signer.py:145` (7 drawings). Neighbouring cases replaced such floors with set comparisons (#517, `conftest.py:committed_round_records_on_disk`).
- **Some sections are found by a heading a person wrote, looked up by exact text.** `## Report` is looked up in `test_a_question_says_who_can_answer_it.py:117`, `test_the_record_is_generated.py:1791/2211/2245/2286`, `test_the_run_stops_at_the_last_finding.py` and `test_the_reviewers_report_reaches_the_record.py`. Several of these read to the end of the file rather than to the next heading. The reopening `###` heading is looked up in three test files. The bilingual `### Shared or local` / `### shared 인가 local 인가` headings are looked up in two. Renaming any one of these headings fails every test that looks it up, usually with a ValueError or IndexError rather than an assertion message.
- **Some pins fail on layout, not wording.** Raw unflattened text carrying a hard wrap inside the pinned phrase: `test_absence_claims.py:25`; `test_both_editions_carry_the_same_folds.py:180` (`\n  edition`); `test_a_folded_statement_names_what_enforces_it.py:91-99`; `test_a_probe_does_not_outlive_its_round.py:27-52`; `test_broad_gate_rule.py:47-56`; Re-wrapping the paragraph fails these tests with no change in meaning.
- **Surprises.** `fold-check` is run by no workflow. `test_a_document_has_room_for_the_next_fold.py` is its only CI enforcement. `test_evals_layout.py:case_dirs` has no floor, so deleting `evals/` passes all five cases. `tests/block_shapes.py` contains no reader. Its `RENDERER` column is a hand-derived expectation that `test_the_hooks_hide_what_a_renderer_hides.py` holds the CommonMark oracle to. The one deliberate differential test, holding three fence implementations equal, is `test_unverified_rows_close.py:2306`. It runs fixtures, so it has no row here.
